+ "#cutout Abandoning an invocation with no cutout to come out of"

+ "#cutout Creating a new menu bar controller"

+ "#cutout Declining an interactive launch; the cutout is not ours to grow out of"
+ "#cutout Discarding a settled collapse rather than reversing it"
+ "#cutout Ignoring a focus-loss dismissal; the island is hidden behind whatever owns the cutout"

+ "#cutout Ignoring the first request to reverse a focus-loss collapse"
+ "#cutout Ignoring the long-press shortcut while ownership is suspended"
+ "#cutout Invalidated takeover client controller"
+ "#cutout Leaving a dismissal in the cutout rather than expanding over it"

+ "#cutout Menu bar controller invalidated (reason %{public}ld)"
+ "#cutout Menu bar ended the session; dropping ownership"
+ "#cutout Menu bar refused the cutout"
+ "#cutout Menu bar takeover unsupported on this device; not connecting"
+ "#cutout Ownership suspended; assertion still held"
+ "#cutout Refusing ownership %{public}s → %{public}s"
+ "#cutout Releasing a parked platter for the transient canvas"
+ "#cutout Reversing an in-flight collapse %{public}s"
+ "#cutout Simulating ownership for %{public}s"
